    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];

    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;

        }
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

assistant: ```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

                return i;
        return -1;

public class AllUnique {
    public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {

                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

public class AllUnique {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == true;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;

assistant: ```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

    public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return true;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;

        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i+1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; i+1 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k];
            //@ maintaining \forall int p; 0 < p < i; \forall int q; p < q < i; arr[p] != arr[q];

public class AllUnique {
    //@ requires arr == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == (\exists int p; 0 < p < arr.length; \forall int q; p <= q <= arr.length; arr[p] != arr[q]);  
    public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return true;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int p; 0 < p < i; \exists int q; p < q <= i; arr[p] != arr[q];  
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i+1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; i+1 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k];
            //@ maintaining \forall int p; 0 < p <= i; \forall int q; p <= q <= i; arr[p] != arr[q];  
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ also
    //@ requires arr != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == (\exists int p; 0 <= p < arr.length; \forall int q; p <= q < arr.length; arr[p] != arr[q]);  
    public static boolean allUnique(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null) return true;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int p; 0 < p < i; \exists int q; p < q <= i; arr[p] != arr[q];  
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i+1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; i+1 <= k < j; arr[i] != arr[k];
            //@ maintaining \forall int p; 0 < p <= i; \forall int q; p <= q <= i; arr[p] != arr[q];

                    return false;

        return true;
    }
